                                                                                               Joseph Perry, Grand Blane,

           &#39; 141-.Josephram, -3079 EdwardsStreet, Grand Blanc,.

          iohigan, was contacted at his request by agents of this office
         on M5a, rch 1960. .

                                                        Hr. Perry atdhavtised                  he operates a Pizza House                                                                    _
         a3t075 Edwards Street, Grand Blane, and that he has been a

         professional photographer for thirty years. Be explained
         that he has a hobby of taking photographs of the moon through

         a home-made telescope. At about 1:00 a.m. on February 21,
         1960, he took some photographs of the moon and after developing
                                                                                               viewer and in one photograph                               la-
         same, he put them in hie                                                              be a flying object somewhere
         observed what atoppears                                                                                                                          between

         the end of his telescope and the suface                                                                                           of the moon. Be said

         that when blown up, this object is folant                                                                                               the bottom, ius

         oval shaped, and appears to have a fluorescent glow around

         the entire object, and that it is apparently moving as it
         has a vapor trail running behind it.- He has taken over one

         thousand pictures of the moon and hnaesver                                                                                              seen anything

         resembling this object.

                                                        Perry said that due to his thirty years"experience
         in photograph, he is sure that what appears on the picture

         is actually a flying object, and is not a malfuction                                                                                             of the

         camera, is not a b1ur»on his telescope, and is not anything
         that appears on the photo because of_tho minanner
                                                                                                                                                          which he

         developed the film; Yerry took about twenty pictures of the
         moon on February 21, 1960, and after developing them, he
         observed that most of the pictures were under-exposed, so he

         jucustt                             off the two that appeared to be properly exposed and

                       On Uecember l7, 1969 the Soef cretary                              the Air Force    .

           announced the toefrmination             Project Blue Book, the Air F_orce

           program for the ionfvestigation                                   UFOs.                              i

                       The decision to discontinue UFO investigations was based

           on an eovfaluation         a report prepared by the Uonf iversity

           Colorado entitled,         Scientific Sotfudy                            Unidentified Flying

           Objects;&#39a; roefview    the Uonf iversity                             Colorado&#39r;esport by
           the National Aocf ademy Sciences: past UFO studies: and Air

           Force experience inUvFeOstigating                                 reports during the past
           two decades.                                                                           &; #39;

                       As a roefsult  these investigations and studies, and

           experience gained from inUvFeOstigating                                  reports since 1948,
           the coofnclusions          PBrloujeect  Book are: ! no UFO reported,

           investigated, and evaluated by the Air Force has ever given

           any ionfdication           threat to our rational security: ! there
           has been no evidence submitted to or discovered by the Air

           Force that sightings categorized as "unidentified" represent

           technological developments or principles beyond the roafnge
           present day scientific knowledge; and ! there has been no

           evidence indicating that sightings categorized as unidentified

           are extraterrestrial       vehicles.

                       with the toefrmination      Project Blue Book, the Air Force

           regulation establishing and controlling the program for invest-

           igating and analyzing UFOs was rescinded. All documentation

           regarding the former Blue Book investigation has been per-

           manently transferred to the Modern Military Branch, National
           Archives and Records Service, 8th and Pennsylvania A&v#e3n9u; e,

           WDa.Cs.hington,            20408, and is available for public review

        - and analysis.

                       Attached for your information is the Froject Blue Book

           sighting summary for the period 1947-1969. Also included is,

           a loisfting           UFO-related materials currently available.

                       Since the toefrmination     Project Blue Book, no evidence

           has been presented to indicate that further inofVeStigatiOn
           UFOs by the Air Force is warranted. In voief w                                 the consider-&#39;

           able Air Force coofmmitment             resources in the past, and the

           extreme pressure on AFiorrce             funds at this time, there is

           no Likelihood         OI renewed-Air    Force                     involvement  in Care1aS.
